  (function() {

      djab_eventQueue = [];

      function handleOptimizelyMetric(metric){
        if (typeof metric === 'object'){
          if (window.optimizelyEdge){
            window.optimizelyEdge.push(metric);
          } else {
            djab_eventQueue.push(metric);
          }
        }
      }

      function handleQueuedOptimizelyMetrics(){
        let len = djab_eventQueue.length;
        while (len--) {
            if (typeof djab_eventQueue[len] === 'object'){
              window.optimizelyEdge.push(djab_eventQueue[len]);
            }
        }
      }

      window.djab_handleOptimizelyMetric = handleOptimizelyMetric;
      window.djab_handleQueuedOptimizelyMetrics = handleQueuedOptimizelyMetrics;
      performance.mark('djab-available-metricHandler');

  })();

(max-width: 979px) 620px, (max-width: 1299px) 540px, 700px" srcset="https://opinion-images.wsj.net/im-89324408/?size=1.5 1280w, https://opinion-images.wsj.net/im-89324408/OR 1280w" width="1280" height="853" src="https://opinion-images.wsj.net/im-89324408/?size=1.5" class="css-1hzqsjo"></picture></figure><figcaption class="eihhvrm0 css-1n6fay2-FigcaptionItem e11lw8gk21"><span class="e1m33gv80 css-426zcb-CaptionSpan e1m33gv81"></span> <span class="css-7jz429-Credit eq0esvu0">Dado Ruvic<!-- -->/<wbr>Reuters</span></figcaption></div><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">The Office of the Comptroller of the Currency issued a preliminary conditional approval last week to establish World Liberty Trust Co., which will be a national trust bank responsible for issuing USD1, a stablecoin. </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">This approval is an important first step toward opening a federally chartered trust bank that can issue USD1 pursuant to the Genius Act of 2025. We look forward to working with the OCC to satisfy the legal and operational requirements necessary for final approval. </p><div class="paywall css-1u1nl00-PaywalledContentContainer e1qcjy9n0"><div class="uds-ad-container e1pa4qj41 css-zsypcb-Container e1opctqs12" data-testid="ad-container"><div class="uds-ad-stack css-efqjdh"><p class="body-ad-label css-1hsjl1g" data-testid="ad-container-label">Advertisement</p><div data-testid="ad-block" class="e1pa4qj40 css-16bhsh0-Content"><div id="wrapper-wsj-body-AD_INLINEMOBILE_1" class="adWrapper " style="min-height: 250px; min-width: 300px;"><div>
        <div id="wsj-body-AD_INLINEMOBILE_1" class="adContainer   staticAd" style=""></div>
    
          <script>
            (function () {

        </div></div></div></div></div><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">Since its release in March 2025, the USD1 stablecoin has become the fourth-largest in the world, with an aggregate market value of about $4 billion. Upon receiving a formal national-bank charter from the OCC, World Liberty will be able to issue and redeem USD1, manage the reserve backing USD1, and provide custody services to customers. A national bank and the federal oversight that comes with it will allow for greater transparency and increase confidence in USD1. In short, final approval will be good for USD1 and good for our customers. </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">Sen. Elizabeth Warren (D., Mass.) a critic of the crypto industry, is outraged, having already accused World Liberty of “unprecedented crypto corruption.” She calls it a “sham” and claims to have “no confidence” in the OCC’s ability to oversee our application impartially. </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">She isn’t alone. Sen. Richard Blumenthal (D., Conn.) said the preliminary conditional approval for World Liberty “puts our banking system in danger,” while Sen. Jack Reed (D., R.I.) accused the Trump family of “gaming the system to enrich itself at the expense of ordinary taxpayers.” </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">The most insidious of these allegations—that World Liberty received preferential treatment at the behest of the Trump administration—doesn’t stand up to scrutiny. For starters, the OCC issued preliminary conditional approvals for stablecoin banking services to five of our competitors nine months before granting conditional approval to our company. </p><div class="uds-ad-container e1pa4qj41 css-zsypcb-Container e1opctqs12" data-testid="ad-container"><div class="uds-ad-stack css-efqjdh"><p class="body-ad-label css-1hsjl1g" data-testid="ad-container-label">Advertisement</p><div data-testid="ad-block" class="e1pa4qj40 css-16bhsh0-Content"><div id="wrapper-wsj-body-AD_INLINEMOBILE_2" class="adWrapper " style="min-height: 250px; min-width: 300px;"><div>
        <div id="wsj-body-AD_INLINEMOBILE_2" class="adContainer   staticAd" style=""></div>
    
          <script>
            (function () {

        </div></div></div></div></div><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">Ms. Warren’s claim that Jonathan Gould, the comptroller of the currency, can’t be trusted to rule impartially isn’t only baseless; it’s irrelevant. As per the OCC, “approvals of applications such as this are made under authority delegated by the Comptroller to career staff.” Lastly, as part of the application process with the OCC, DT Marks, the Trump family holding company that owns a stake in World Liberty Financial, entered into detailed passivity commitments with the OCC, setting forth steps to assure it wouldn’t exercise any control over World Liberty.</p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">Our critics are missing the point. World Liberty is running toward regulation and supervision, not away from it. A national charter locks in rigorous permanent supervision by the OCC, which is exactly what Congress intended when it passed the Genius Act. This oversight will outlast President Trump’s time in office. For years to come, World Liberty will undergo routine examinations of its books, risk management and internal controls. It will publish weekly reports on USD1 use, reserves and operational stability, each subject to independent verification. Federal banking laws, including Bank Secrecy Act requirements and sanctions obligations, will apply and be enforceable against World Liberty and its management.</p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">The OCC’s preliminary conditional approval of our charter is good for USD1 and our customers. More important, the continued expansion of stablecoins is good for the U.S. Stablecoin use is increasing demand for U.S. Treasurys. As economist and former Federal Reserve Gov. Stephen Miran said in a speech last year, widespread stablecoin adoption would lower interest rates. There will be individual and business benefits too. Stablecoins like USD1 are making capital more accessible and financial transactions faster and more frictionless. Stablecoins will democratize a consolidated industry and protect customers from debanking while giving them a safe, simple means of storing their assets. </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">With the passage of the Genius Act in 2025, Congress affirmed these benefits and laid out a regulatory framework. Many Senate Democrats supported the bill. It’s disappointing to see some of the same senators—Angela Alsobrooks (D., Md.), Ruben Gallego (D. Ariz.), Andy Kim (D., N.J.)—who recognized the importance of integrating stablecoins into our financial system in 2025, now attack World Liberty for helping achieve an outcome they voted for. </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0">My hope is that shortsighted partisanship doesn’t become a long-term impediment to a freer, more accessible and better functioning financial system. </p><p data-type="paragraph" data="[object Object]" class="css-1qg6i2u ex0k10u0"><em data-type="emphasis" class="css-i6hrxa-Italic e1ofiv6m0"> Mr. Witkoff is CEO of World Liberty Financial.</em></p><div data-type="inset" data-inset_type="dynamic" data-sub_type="series-navigation" class="media-layout css-13plya0-Layout-baseCss ertdlv30" data-layout="inline"><div     function loadCSS(href, before, media) {
      var ss = window.document.createElement("link");
      var ref = before || window.document.getElementsByTagName("meta")[0];
      var sheets = window.document.styleSheets;
      ss.rel = "stylesheet";
      ss.href = href.trim();
      ss.media = "only x";
      ref.parentNode.appendChild(ss, ref);
      function toggleMedia() {
        var defined;
        for (var i = 0; i < sheets.length; i++) {
          if (sheets[i].href && sheets[i].href.indexOf(href.trim().substr(-8)) > -1) {
            defined = true;
          }
        }
        if (defined) {
          ss.media = media || "all";
        }
        else {
          setTimeout(toggleMedia);
        }
      }

      toggleMedia();
      return ss;
    }

    window.loadCSS = loadCSS;
    </script><script>
    function loadJs(src) {
      var ref = window.document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0];
      var script = window.document.createElement("script");
      script.src = src;
      script.defer = 1;
      ref.parentNode.insertBefore(script, ref);
      return script;
    }
    window.loadJs = loadJs
    </script><script>
    function whenAvailable(name, callback, ms) {
      const interval = ms ? ms : 15; // ms
      var timeout = window.setTimeout(function () {
        if (window[name]) {
          window.clearTimeout(timeout);
          callback(window[name]);
        } else {
          window.setTimeout(arguments.callee, interval);
        }
      }, interval);
    }
    window.whenAvailable = whenAvailable;

    (function() {
      function deferredPromise() {
        let _resolve;
        let _reject;

        const promise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          _resolve = resolve;
          _reject = reject;
        });

        promise.resolve = _resolve;
        promise.reject = _reject;

        return promise;
      }

      function decodeJWT(token) {
        try {
          const base64Url = token.split('.')[1];
          const base64 = base64Url.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/') + '='.repeat((4 - base64Url.length % 4) % 4);
          const jsonPayload = decodeURIComponent(atob(base64).split('').map(function (c) {
            return '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2);
          }).join(''));
          return JSON.parse(jsonPayload);
        } catch (e) {
          return null;
        }
      }

      function getCookieValue(name) {
        const cookiePair = document.cookie
          .split('; ')
          .find((row) => row.startsWith(name + '='));

        if (!cookiePair) return null;

        const encodedValue = cookiePair.split('=')[1];
        try {
          return decodeURIComponent(encodedValue);
        } catch (e) {
          return encodedValue;
        }
      }

      function getValidPotFromURL() {
        const queryParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const pot = queryParams.get('pot');
        if (!pot) return null;

        const potObject = decodeJWT(pot);
        if (!potObject?.ruleId || !potObject?.exp || !Number.isFinite(potObject?.exp)) return null;
        if (potObject.exp <= Date.now() / 1000) return null;

        const ca_r = getCookieValue('ca_r');
        if (!ca_r) return pot;

        const ruleIdFromCookie = ca_r?.split('|')[2];
        if (!ruleIdFromCookie || ruleIdFromCookie === String(potObject.ruleId)) {
          return null;
        }

        return pot;
      }

      async function fetchOffers() {
        window.__cxdj_init = deferredPromise();
        const entitlementsAsyncData = await window.entitlementsAsyncData;
        const trackid = entitlementsAsyncData?.trackid || '';
        const offersCacheVersion = entitlementsAsyncData?.offersCacheVersion;

        if (!true) {
          window.__cxdj_init?.resolve();
          return;
        }

        const pot = getValidPotFromURL();

        const oldOffersStr = localStorage.getItem('ca_offers');
        let oldOffers;
        let jwt = '';

        try {
          if (oldOffersStr && !pot) {
            oldOffers = JSON.parse(oldOffersStr);
            const isSameUser = trackid === oldOffers?.trackid;
            const storedVersion = oldOffers?.cacheVersion ?? '1';
            const isValidVersion = storedVersion === offersCacheVersion;

            if (isSameUser && isValidVersion && oldOffers?.exp > Date.now()) {
              await window.__utagReady;
              window.__cxdj_init?.resolve();
              return;
            }

          // Extend the jwt in case CAJ pricing fetch fails
          if (isSameUser) {
              jwt = oldOffers?.jwt;
            }
          }
        } catch {}

        const offersParams = new URLSearchParams();
        if (jwt) offersParams.set('jwt', jwt);

        const url = '/client/offers' + (offersParams.toString() ? '?' + offersParams.toString() : '');

        try {
          const FETCH_TIMEOUT = 1000;
          const controller = new AbortController();
          const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), FETCH_TIMEOUT);

          const fetchOptions = {
            cache: 'no-cache',
            credentials: 'include',
            signal: controller.signal,
            headers: {
              'x-original-referrer': window.document.referrer,
              'x-original-url': window?.location?.pathname + window?.location?.search + window?.location?.hash,
              'x-original-host': window?.location?.host,
            }
          };

          const response = await fetch(url, fetchOptions);

          clearTimeout(timeoutId);

          if (!response.ok) {
            window.localStorage.removeItem('ca_offers');
            window.__cxdj_init?.resolve();
            return;
          }

          let data = await response.json();

          // if extendedJwt is present, the pricing offers fetch fails and the existing jwt was extended
          if (data?.extendedJwt) {
            data = { ...oldOffers, exp: data.exp, jwt: data.extendedJwt };
          }

          window.localStorage.setItem('ca_offers', JSON.stringify(data));
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Error fetching offers', e);
        }

        await window.__utagReady;
        window.__cxdj_init?.resolve();
      }

      fetchOffers();
    })();
